# Skills · G · part 71

905 entries, graveyard-historian to grefcoco-eval. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **graveyard-historian**: When pitching an idea, researches the graveyard — companies that tried something similar in the past 5–25 years and failed, what specifically killed them, and which operators or investors lived through those failures and would be valuable to talk to. Use before pitching to a VC, a customer, or a hiring manager — the graveyard reframes the pitch from "this is a great idea" to "this is a great idea AND I know why every previous attempt died." Triggers on "research the graveyard," "who tried this before," "previous attempts," "failed companies in this space," "post-mortem," "why did X fail." ([kalyvask/winning-writing](https://github.com/kalyvask/winning-writing/tree/HEAD/skills/graveyard-historian) · ★18)
- **graveyard-killer**: Capture a shared post into the user's Graveyard Killer Library, and recall what the user saved there. Use whenever a message is a link (Instagram, TikTok, YouTube or any web page) with no request, such as a bare link, a link inside an app's share text, or a link with a short "save this"; whenever the user asks about a post they saved; and whenever the user names Graveyard Killer or answers one of its messages, for example to set it up, import old saves, get its Digest or reply to its offers. ([andreadorizza/graveyard-killer](https://github.com/andreadorizza/graveyard-killer/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/graveyard-killer) · ★9)
- **graveyard-killer-worker**: Run the Graveyard Killer Worker on this computer, which reads the posts waiting in the user's Library, finishes their Entries and writes the Ping for the user. Use when a message asks to run the Graveyard Killer worker or its tidy-up, as its scheduled task does, and pass that whole message as the argument. Give the user its final message as it is: it is the Ping. ([andreadorizza/graveyard-killer](https://github.com/andreadorizza/graveyard-killer/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/graveyard-killer-worker) · ★9)
